C1 Coach body · Parts & assembly← DrawingsRotate C1Calculations & evidence ↗
C1 regular coach body
Physical body and bracket proposal · R0 · 19 October 2026 · dimensions in inches unless marked
The coach is still unfinished. These are new physical body/roof parts and a proposed interior post connection. Seats remain space references. Actual trucks, brake mounts, stairs, couplers, HVAC mounts and many enclosure connections still need design. No construction release.
Framing cutaway from exported solids.Enclosure geometry; attachment and finish detail incomplete.
Drawings and fabrication source
Body arrangement · Assembly cutaway · Post/frame joint · Web blank / holes
Full solid STEP (mm · gzip) · J01 solid STEP (mm) · gzip · Editable CAD builder · Parameters · Web cutting coordinates
J01 preliminary assembly sequence
Survey actual tube outside widths and corner profiles. Trial-fit the cap and connection on an unoccupied prototype; four end-post receivers require a separate design.
Cut the proposed web/backing/shim/rib blanks. Machine sleeves and jig-bore aligned holes through both HSS walls. No sleeve press fit or bolt tightening value is released.
Trial-fit the sleeve/backing/web stack. Interior bracket nominally clears adjacent crossmembers by 0.5 in; check actual welds, tools, stock tolerance and access.
Develop and qualify the proposed rib/web weld sequence and sleeve retention, including distortion, fatigue and inspection requirements. Verify cap/sill contact and effective end welds against actual corners.
Check panel notches and sealed removable closeouts before installing floor or skin. Record actual hardware stack, wrench access, fit and inspection results before coating.
Precision machining and finish work are planned for subcontracting. General cut/fit work requires demonstrated shop capability and later released details.
